makes at run time (path rewrites, upload switches, pip modes, environment) are not in any `stage.ipy`; they are listed once in
[`runner/RUNTIME_CHANGES.md`](../runner/RUNTIME_CHANGES.md). Change types follow DESIGN 2.5: `path`, `url`, `upload`,

## env_docker

`stages/00_env/env_docker/CHANGES.md` (00_env, new)

### env_docker: changes with respect to the original

Origin: `new` (DESIGN 2.3 rank 7). There is no original run: the stage is new code. Its reference input is the Dockerfile of the
team repository, `release/docker/Dockerfile`, copied **unchanged** to `original/Dockerfile` and to `docker/Dockerfile`
(SHA-256 `ad84e6fea5f55541c087d85fe5de806049d2f93fe0a3a5a1530842fbbbe66eb9`, identical to `E:/FLAG2027_submit/FACTOR_ENFUSE_c5rl/docker/Dockerfile`).

| # | where | old -> new | reason | effect on numbers | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | whole stage | DESIGN 4.0 "build the image" -> "check the environment against the pins; print the docker build commands" | advisor's decision 18/11: Docker is not required, no new Docker work; the environment specification (base image digest + pip pins) is kept | none (no stage of the chain reads its output) | `pin` |
| 2 | `docker/Dockerfile` last line `CMD ["python", "/opt/fourfusion/docker/ff.py", "plan"]` | kept verbatim | the Dockerfile is a reference copy; `docker/ff.py` belonged to the older U′ package and is not part of this repository. Start containers with an explicit command (`docker/README.md` section 3) | none | none |
| 3 | `docker/constraints-kaggle-v173.txt`, `docker/requirements-kaggle-pinned.txt` | new files | the Dockerfile pins written as a pip constraints file (used by `FF_PIP=pinned`) and as an installable list | none | `pin` |
| 4 | `stage.ipy` cell 3, `original_runs` | "python 3.13.16 (GPU runs; one CPU run 3.13.18)" -> "GPU image 3.13.16, CPU image 3.13.18 (November runs; older images in Sept / Oct)", torch 2.11.0+cu128 / 2.10.0+cu128 added for the Sept / Oct runs | review 18/11: every kept CPU log prints 3.13.18 and the collection / early feature runs used older images (docker/ENV_PER_STAGE.md, configs/env_per_stage.json) | none (report text) | `pin` |

The stage writes `pip_freeze.txt` (every installed distribution, read with `importlib.metadata` so that it also works when pip is
replaced by the no-op shim of `FF_PIP=skip`) and `env_report.json`.

## models_fetch

`stages/00_env/models_fetch/CHANGES.md` (00_env, new)

### models_fetch: changes with respect to the original

Origin: `new` (DESIGN 2.3 rank 7). The reference code is `cmd_fetch_models` of `release/docker/ff.py` (team repository; file copied
unchanged to `original/ff.py`, SHA-256 `c706b0b729b3b77961515d6e888fe9a8a567ea64dd220a821ad81bc64a06d8d2`, lines 286–306), which
downloads ImageBind-huge from the pinned Hugging Face copy and keeps it only when its SHA-256 is Meta's. The stage runs
`tools/fetch_models.py` (new) and streams its output line by line.

| # | where | old -> new | reason | effect on numbers | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | model list | ImageBind only -> the full table of DESIGN 9.2 / S8 E10 (25 entries in four groups: core, dp, lid, wiring) | offline runs and a pinned record of every public model | none: same files the notebooks download (SHA-256 checked where measured) | `pin` |
| 2 | HF downloads | the notebooks call `from_pretrained` / `snapshot_download` with the default revision `main` (ViT-age, audeering, AdaFace, CLIP-L in places, SpeechBrain) -> `tools/fetch_models.py` downloads `snapshot_download(revision=<commit>)` into `06_pretrained/cache/huggingface/hub` and then writes `models--<repo>/refs/main = <commit>`, so that the notebooks' unchanged `main` lookups resolve to the pinned commit when the runner points `HF_HUB_CACHE` at that cache. Pinned only with `FF_HF_OFFLINE=1` (HF_HUB_OFFLINE): online, huggingface_hub re-resolves `main` on the Hub and may fetch a newer commit | `main` can move; the commits are the ones the team caches hold (E: package `pretrained/cache/huggingface/hub/*/refs/main`, team machine cache) | none when the pinned commit is used (offline); online runs may differ if a repository changed after the original run | `pin` |
| 3 | WeSpeaker ModelScope zips | the notebooks fetch them through the ModelScope tree API at run time -> the same API, then unpacked like `wespeaker_dir()` into `cache/flag/wespeaker/<stem>/` | lets `FLAG_CACHE` skip the download | none (zip SHA-256 checked: values measured on `D:/ff_models/`, 2026-11-18) | `pin` |
| 4 | ImageBind URL | Meta's `dl.fbaipublicfiles.com` (dead since 2026-11-12) -> HF `PKU-Alignment/AnyRewardModel@40c7e2a` | dead link; same SHA-256 `d6f6c22b…2157` | none | `url` |
| 5 | smoke mode | — -> `FF_FETCH_DRY=1` lists the table and writes `PLAN.txt` + a placeholder `SOURCES.json` | smoke test without network | none | — |

## raw_btc

`stages/10_raw/raw_btc/CHANGES.md` (10_raw, new)

### CHANGES: `raw_btc`

Stage raw_btc (10_raw): Check the organisers' MAV-Celeb v4 train / dev release (no producing code).

Origin: new.

| # | where | old -> new | why | effect on numbers | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | cells/00_check.py (whole stage) | new verification cell (no original code: organisers' data) | prove that FF_DATA holds the exact release the system read | none (reads only) | new |

#### Notes

- The stage has no artifact output the chain consumes: raw data is always taken from FF_DATA (DESIGN §2.4); raw_btc only checks it.
- dev_set.zip is reported if present and never required: no stage of the 13.54 chain reads dev data (DESIGN §2.8).

#### How stage.ipy is built

Recipe `ff-stage-ipy/1` (same convention as `release/flag2027_e2e/build_notebook.py:stage_cells`): take the cells listed in `stage.json` `build.sequence` in that order (`orig`: index of the cell in the original notebook's `nb["cells"]` list, markdown cells counted, and the referenced cell must be a non-empty code cell, as in `runner/check_stage.py`; CRLF -> LF; `new`: a file of `cells/`), apply every `patches` entry to the original cells only (each must match exactly `count` times in total), and join every cell as `cell.rstrip('\n') + '\n# <<<FF-CELL>>>\n'`. The sha256 of the result is `build.stage_ipy_sha256`. Original code cells not in the sequence are the ones matched by `drop_cells` (regex on the cell title = its `print("[cell] ...")` text or first line).

## r07_overlap

`stages/12_rows/r07_overlap/CHANGES.md` (12_rows, local-script)

### CHANGES: `r07_overlap`

Stage r07_overlap (12_rows): Evidence: MAV <-> v4 identity overlap list (SET-07), train-only rule; the chain hard-codes the result.

Origin: local-script (`Experiment/SET-07/overlap.py`).

| # | where | old -> new | why | effect on numbers | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | overlap.py lines 4-10 | R / 'kaggle', R / 'kaggle_upload', R / 'kaggle/output/feats_ext_full', R / 'kaggle/output/feats_v2', cwd overlap.csv -> --lib, --data, --feats-ext, --feats-dir (default = --feats-ext), --out | team-machine paths | none (feats_v2 = FLAG_04 output holds no array this script reads: FeatureStore takes ext_* from feats_ext_full and the faces from the organiser csv) | path |
| 2 | overlap.py, new flag --train-only (default of the stage) | skip lines 24-27 (THR_DEV from the 20 nearest dev faces) and the dev term of line 36-37; stub flag_lib.load_dev so FeatureStore reads no dev file | DESIGN §4.3 / G11: no dev data in the reproduction. With --train-only the list depends only on THR, the v4-train centroid threshold. The SET-07 run did not save THR; the same rule printed THR = 0.624 in FLAG_08 (EXPERIMENT_LOG entry 37; dev threshold 0.659). Derivation from the original output: the dev rule flagged nobody (the highest top20_cos_dev, 0.6039, is not flagged, so THR_DEV >= 0.6039, and both flagged persons have top20_cos_dev <= 0.513), so both were flagged by the centroid rule: THR < 0.6503 (v1_complete:shaukat_tarin, flagged) and THR >= 0.6212 (v2_complete:id0060, not flagged); 0.898 (v2_complete:id0073) is far above. THR in [0.6212, 0.6503) gives the same list without the dev term | none on the list with the original arrays. Margin is small (0.624 - 0.6212 = 0.003): VGG-Face arrays regenerated on another GPU (mode a) could move a person across THR; cell 01 prints THR and both margins and only warns when every differing person is within 0.01 of THR (the chain hard-codes the list) | dev-exclusion |
| 3 | lib/flag_v2.py, lib/flag_lib.py | copies of git a89a071 kaggle/flag_v2.py and kaggle/flag_lib.py (the run imported the working copy of 27/09) | the stage folder must be self-contained | none: FeatureStore.__init__, ext_source, load_train, load_dev, find_file, l2n, NAMES are identical (ast) in the executed FLAG_08 copy (26/09), a89a071 (29/09) and today's working copy | pin |
| 4 | cells/00_data.py, cells/01_overlap.py | new: unpack the three organiser files from train_set.zip (+ dev csv / lists only with R07_WITH_DEV=1), run overlap.py, compare with the hard-coded list | run inside the stage runner | none | new |
| 5 | cells/00_data.py, cells/01_overlap.py | the organiser csv files (r07_data/) and overlap.csv are written to a tempfile.mkdtemp folder outside /kaggle/working and deleted after the check (they were in /kaggle/working, so the runner saved them as the kernel output); lines printed by overlap.py that name a v4 train identity (its top-rows table with the v4train_id column) are replaced by a placeholder; r07_check.json keeps MAV person keys, THR, margins and the comparison only | the organisers' data must not end up in a saved run output; overlap.csv pairs every MAV person with its nearest v4 train identity, an identity-linkage table whose use is a pending team decision (docs/COMPLIANCE_NOTES.md) | none (the list is computed from the same table before it is deleted) | new |
| 6 | stage.json `force_only: true` | the stage runs only when named in FF_FORCE (FF_FORCE=r07_overlap) | evidence only (nothing consumes it: the chain hard-codes the two keys) and the linkage decision is pending | none | new |

#### Notes

- R07_WITH_DEV=1 reruns the original rule (reads dev feature csv files and lists from dev_set.zip); not used for the 13.54 reproduction.
- The original overlap.csv (team machine) is not copied into the repo and the stage keeps no such table: it pairs each MAV person with its nearest v4 train identity (a linkage table). original/SOURCE.json records only the two flagged MAV keys and their max_cos_v4train values.
- expected.json `threshold` holds THR = 0.624 (log 37), the bracket [0.6212, 0.6503) and its derivation.

#### How stage.ipy is built

Recipe `ff-stage-ipy/1` (same convention as `release/flag2027_e2e/build_notebook.py:stage_cells`): take the cells listed in `stage.json` `build.sequence` in that order (`orig`: index of the cell in the original notebook's `nb["cells"]` list, markdown cells counted, and the referenced cell must be a non-empty code cell, as in `runner/check_stage.py`; CRLF -> LF; `new`: a file of `cells/`), apply every `patches` entry to the original cells only (each must match exactly `count` times in total), and join every cell as `cell.rstrip('\n') + '\n# <<<FF-CELL>>>\n'`. The sha256 of the result is `build.stage_ipy_sha256`. Original code cells not in the sequence are the ones matched by `drop_cells` (regex on the cell title = its `print("[cell] ...")` text or first line).

- DESIGN N1 says this copy equals `git show 8544b3b:kaggle/FLAG_08_external.ipynb`: checked 18/11, it does not (code cells 2, 4, 8 and markdown 0 differ from 8544b3b; cells 2 and 4 differ from a89a071). The executed copy is used as is.
- Cell 2 embeds flag_lib.py, flag_extract.py, flag_v2.py as base64; they are decoded to original/decoded/ for reading only (flag_lib.py = git 8544b3b; flag_extract.py and flag_v2.py match no commit).
- extract_external of the executed flag_extract.py has no clip limit and no crop (signature: frames_per_video=8, max_padded_sec=240, max_bs=32, face_bs=64); the meta tables depend only on file names / sizes, so they must come out byte-identical on Linux.
- Kept as in the run: `!pip -q install -U "speechbrain>=1.1.1"` (pip_policy original), the unpinned yangwang825/ecapa-tdnn-vox2 download, the Drive fallback code (never reached when the dataset is attached).
- Runner metadata (no code change): `skip_with` lists only `02_intermediate/feats_ext_full` (the runner takes the first existing skip_with folder as the kernel output; `ext_meta` holds only the four meta tables and is the data_map path of the export); top-level `scratch: [_vggface, _ecapa_btc]` keeps the downloaded model files out of the collected output (they were only under `outputs.scratch`, which the runner does not read).

#### How stage.ipy is built

Recipe `ff-stage-ipy/1` (same convention as `release/flag2027_e2e/build_notebook.py:stage_cells`): take the cells listed in `stage.json` `build.sequence` in that order (`orig`: index of the cell in the original notebook's `nb["cells"]` list, markdown cells counted, and the referenced cell must be a non-empty code cell, as in `runner/check_stage.py`; CRLF -> LF; `new`: a file of `cells/`), apply every `patches` entry to the original cells only (each must match exactly `count` times in total), and join every cell as `cell.rstrip('\n') + '\n# <<<FF-CELL>>>\n'`. The sha256 of the result is `build.stage_ipy_sha256`. Original code cells not in the sequence are the ones matched by `drop_cells` (regex on the cell title = its `print("[cell] ...")` text or first line).

Written as the original (json indent 0, Windows line ends) and compared by sha256 with the embedded copy; same guard policy as the other three files.

- Consumers find the file by name (rglob 'lid_strict.json', T3ENC-01 strict_drop) and check counts == list lengths; they apply it with LID_STRICT=1 (+ BN42=1 for the face adapters).

#### How stage.ipy is built

Recipe `ff-stage-ipy/1` (same convention as `release/flag2027_e2e/build_notebook.py:stage_cells`): take the cells listed in `stage.json` `build.sequence` in that order (`orig`: index of the cell in the original notebook's `nb["cells"]` list, markdown cells counted, and the referenced cell must be a non-empty code cell, as in `runner/check_stage.py`; CRLF -> LF; `new`: a file of `cells/`), apply every `patches` entry to the original cells only (each must match exactly `count` times in total), and join every cell as `cell.rstrip('\n') + '\n# <<<FF-CELL>>>\n'`. The sha256 of the result is `build.stage_ipy_sha256`. Original code cells not in the sequence are the ones matched by `drop_cells` (regex on the cell title = its `print("[cell] ...")` text or first line).

## b_lab_avfull

`stages/20_features/b_lab_avfull/CHANGES.md` (20_features, new)

### CHANGES: stage `b_lab_avfull`

Original code: none kept (exactness `new`). The dataset `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p` was packed on 15/10/2026 18:06 by an ad-hoc script in a Claude scratchpad of the team machine (deleted with the Temp folder) and uploaded at 18:07 as a private Kaggle dataset (kaggle/kapi_runs.jsonl line 300). Stage code: `stage.ipy` (3 new cells, each starting with `print("[cell] ...", flush=True)`).
The runner points every `/kaggle/input` and `/kaggle/working` string at the stage's own input view and working directory; that is not a code change.

#### What the stage rebuilds

| member | made from |
|---|---|
| `labavfull/Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full/ext_v{1,2}_complete_{face,voice}_{aclip,rows}.npy` (8 files) | byte copies of the files of the same name in the output of stage `f32_aclip_full` (FLAG_32 run 2) |
| `labavfull/lab_manifest.json` | `{"created", "kind": "data-avfull", "files": {path: bytes}, "note"}` with the original note text, json indent 1: the keys of the original manifest and of kaggle/build_lab_bundle.py build_vb() (stage b_lab_vb) |

The layout (top folder `labavfull/`, repository-relative paths below it) is the one the consumers' OPTIONAL_DIRS entry `CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full` expects (e112f0sx).

#### Differences from the original dataset

- `labavfull/lab_manifest.json` carries the build time (`created`), so it is never byte-identical (the original says 2026-10-15 18:06); it is not compared.
- The 8 npy files are compared by sha256 with `expected_members.json` (read 19/11/2026 from the package E: zip, sha256 b5febc05...). With the frozen f32 output (or the f32 skip_with view = the frozen `feats_av_full` folder) they are identical (local test 19/11/2026: 8 / 8 identical, guard pass). After a GPU re-run of FLAG_32 the AudioCLIP arrays differ; the row files must not.

#### Guard (stage.json `guard_policy`, read by cell 3)

| difference in | FF_MODE=train | FF_MODE=infer / assemble / unset |
|---|---|---|
| `*_aclip.npy` | warn (FLAG_32 is a GPU run, not bit-identical when re-run) | fail |
| `*_rows.npy` | fail (arange(N) of the ext-meta tables: other rows = misaligned arrays) | fail |

`FF_ALLOW_MISMATCH=1` or `FF_SMOKE=1` turn every failure into a warning. Same policy family as l_groups / l_dqual_g3 / l_lid_audit / l_lid_strict. The decision and the list of differing files are written to `bundle_report.json` (`guard`).

#### Run checks (stage.json post_checks / verify)

- cell 2 (fatal outside smoke): row files int64 arange(N) with N = 7 580 / 19 556 / 8 953 / 20 715 (ext-meta rows); AudioCLIP arrays float16 (N, 1024) and finite.
- post_check (fatal): `bundle_report.json` `vs_original/missing` = [] and `structure/ok` = true.
- verify (warning only): `vs_original/extra` = [], `vs_original/differ` = [].

#### Notes

- Private: features of MAV-Celeb v1 / v2 (organisers' data, challenge terms); shared only with the organisers through FF_DATA, never in the repository.
- Wiring only: e112f0sx reads these arrays on the F0 English path (EN-T3-01 table_en), which reaches f1s_eval.zip but not the 13.54 zip.

The first run's notebook (md5 436c24d6...) is not available, so the equality of its split / MAV code with r2's is not verified; those arrays only fill the samS4 slot that v_cat4 drops.
- **dev-exclusion** (environment, no code change): stage.json env pins `FLAG_SPLITS=train`. The original code lets FLAG_SPLITS override the patched SPLIT_FILTER in a smoke / local run; the runner applies the stage env after the caller's environment, so no outside value can turn dev extraction back on. Same value as the patched default: effect on numbers none.
- **dev-exclusion** (input view, pending runner support): the raw-v4 input asks the runner not to mount `dev_set.zip` / `dev_set` (stage.json inputs[].exclude). Today runner/ffcore.py build_vin applies only the configs/data_map.json exclude, so the archive is still visible; the code never unpacks or reads it (ZIP_PREFIXES / train-only index). With raw-v4 mounted unpacked (a hand-attached Kaggle dataset), find_splits still reads the dev list files (pd.read_csv of the list and an existence check of its media) before SPLIT_FILTER drops them; no dev clip or image is decoded or encoded. Mount raw-v4 as the zipped organiser release (FF_DATA/00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4).
- **dev-exclusion** (consequence of SPLIT_FILTER): the encoder self-checks (`self_check`: batch-vs-single and fp16-vs-fp32 cosine > 0.995 on the 3 longest clips of the first non-train split, else of train) now run on train clips; the original took a dev list. Effect on numbers: none (check only). Check behaviour on train clips: untested. A failing check puts the encoder in FAILED and skips it without stopping the run; the post_check on feats_manifest.json `files` below turns that into a stage failure.
- **environment** (unverified assumption): The pushed notebook almost certainly carried a `kapi --env` cell that kaggle/kapi.py does not log (cmd_push inserts smoke_cell(envs, '# run parameters (kapi --env) ...') but the kapi_runs.jsonl line holds only time, account, kernel, source, notebook_md5, gpu, smoke, secrets, datasets, kernels, ok). Evidence: the code reads TAP_UPLOAD (stage.ipy line 891) only from the environment and the output dataset dnq1209/flag2027-feats-tap01-voice-p exists (the dataset of the package-E copy; whether it was created by the upload branch or by hand is not recorded). Other environment knobs the code reads (TAP_AUX_MAX and the other os.environ.get knobs of the notebook) may have been set in the same cell; this stage assumes their defaults. Unverified: re-applying the kapi transform with or without such a cell does not give the pushed md5 e192b1b76ee4 locally.

#### Notes

- samS4 does not enter any score: it only decides which auxiliary rows the CA head trains on (rows with NaN are dropped).

#### Other differences from the original run (inputs, wiring, environment)

- **wiring**: The optional reference inputs (flag2027-feats-v2, flag2027-feats-models) of the check cell are not attached: check_vs_reference.csv stays empty. The optional resume input is not attached.
- **dev-exclusion** (input view, pending runner support): the raw-v4 input asks the runner not to mount `dev_set.zip` / `dev_set` (stage.json inputs[].exclude). Today runner/ffcore.py build_vin applies only the configs/data_map.json exclude, so the archive is still visible; the code never unpacks or reads it (ZIP_PREFIXES / train-only index). With raw-v4 mounted unpacked (a hand-attached Kaggle dataset), find_splits still reads the dev list files (pd.read_csv of the list and an existence check of its media) before SPLIT_FILTER drops them; no dev clip or image is decoded or encoded. Mount raw-v4 as the zipped organiser release (FF_DATA/00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4).
- **dev-exclusion** (consequence of SPLIT_FILTER): the encoder self-checks (`self_check`: batch-vs-single and fp16-vs-fp32 cosine > 0.995 on the 3 longest clips of the first non-train split, else of train) now run on train clips; the original took a dev list. Effect on numbers: none (check only). Check behaviour on train clips: untested. A failing check puts the encoder in FAILED and skips it without stopping the run; the post_check on feats_manifest.json `files` below turns that into a stage failure.

#### Notes

- Today's builder (kaggle/build_eval_notebooks.py, modified after a89a071) adds broken-file handling, a [boot] line and the ImageBind URL (+58/-10 lines). The a89a071 version is used because it is the closest to the run (the run's manifests list no broken file, so the extra handling would not change the arrays).
- The 'FLAG_12 loop' (cells DATA12 / AUDIO12 / ENC12) is the shared data loop of FLAG_40 / 47b / 103c / 160a / 162a / 50cc: v4 and test lists use the whole clip, MAV-Celeb is cut to the centre 12 s (137 MAV v1 files at 44.1 kHz are resampled).
- Two different ECAPA models: speechbrain/spkrec-ecapa-voxceleb (this stage, arrays ecapa192 / ecapa6144, DP only) vs yangwang825/ecapa-tdnn-vox2 (the organisers' ECAPA space, stage r08_mav_rows).

#### Other differences from the original run (inputs, wiring, environment)

- **wiring**: The key lists are read from datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-vox2-keys (face_meta.csv, voice_meta.csv) instead of the FLAG_37 output dataset hthlin/flag2027-feats-vox2 that the run attached: the two files are byte-identical (sha256 abf8cb53... / 3db779d3..., S2 A08). The code finds them by name (rglob face_meta.csv / voice_meta.csv).
- **wiring**: Media are streamed from Hugging Face (gaunernst/voxceleb2-dev-wds tars; ProgramComputer/voxceleb vox2_mp4_{2..6}.zip by HTTP range). `resolve/main` is not pinned (G27).

#### Notes

- Deterministic given the keys: frames are the middle frame of the keyed track, voices the keyed clip (the tar order only changes the visiting order).

#### Code changes

| # | where | type | old -> new (full text in stage.json) | why | effect on numbers |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | cell 1, line 18 | `dev-exclusion` | `SPLIT_FILTER = None            # e.g. ["no_gender/English"]; None = e…` -> `SPLIT_FILTER = ["train"]      # FF dev-exclusion: organiser train lis…` | no dev list is indexed or encoded (DESIGN §2.8; as TRAIN_ONLY of release/flag2027_e2e/stages.py:151) | none on the train / MAV-Celeb / auxiliary arrays (each split is encoded on its own); the dev arrays and dev index files are no longer written |
| 2 | cell 1, line 19 | `dev-exclusion` | `ZIP_PREFIXES = ("train_set", "dev_set", "eval", "test")             #…` -> `ZIP_PREFIXES = ("train_set", "eval", "test")                        #…` | with zipped raw data the original unpacked dev_set.zip too (then SPLIT_FILTER dropped it); now the dev archive is never read | none |
| 3 | cell 2, line 5 | `pin` | `    !pip -q install --no-deps "git+https://github.com/wenet-e2e/wespe…` -> `    # FF environment patch (CHANGES.md): the team's own pip fix of 29…` | on the current Kaggle image the openai CLIP build fails (its setup.py needs pkg_resources) and, in this shared pip call, takes wespeaker down with it (BEST/v1977/README.md §6). Same packages and commits (wespeaker@9fecd6c, CLIP@d50d76d), one pip call each; CLIP from a git clone at d50d76d when the pip build fails | none (same code at the same commits) |

#### Other differences from the original run (inputs, wiring, environment)

- **dev-exclusion** (environment, no code change): stage.json env pins `FLAG_SPLITS=train`. The original code lets FLAG_SPLITS override the patched SPLIT_FILTER in a smoke / local run; the runner applies the stage env after the caller's environment, so no outside value can turn dev extraction back on. Same value as the patched default: effect on numbers none.
- **dev-exclusion** (input view, pending runner support): the raw-v4 input asks the runner not to mount `dev_set.zip` / `dev_set` (stage.json inputs[].exclude). Today runner/ffcore.py build_vin applies only the configs/data_map.json exclude, so the archive is still visible; the code never unpacks or reads it (ZIP_PREFIXES / train-only index). With raw-v4 mounted unpacked (a hand-attached Kaggle dataset), find_splits still reads the dev list files (pd.read_csv of the list and an existence check of its media) before SPLIT_FILTER drops them; no dev clip or image is decoded or encoded. Mount raw-v4 as the zipped organiser release (FF_DATA/00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4).
- **dev-exclusion** (consequence of SPLIT_FILTER): the encoder self-checks (`self_check`: batch-vs-single and fp16-vs-fp32 cosine > 0.995 on the 3 longest clips of the first non-train split, else of train) now run on train clips; the original took a dev list. Effect on numbers: none (check only). Check behaviour on train clips: untested. A failing check puts the encoder in FAILED and skips it without stopping the run; the post_check on feats_manifest.json `files` below turns that into a stage failure.

#### Notes

- Exported dataset = the folder feats_screen2 (the original dataset was created by hand from the same folder on 12/10 12:30).
- Consumer t112sf1s (WP-E): its executed run scored the 4 dev lists with this dataset's dev arrays (Experiment/CLEANTUNE-01/run.py patch_test_cat4). WP-E's default train-only route (FF_TRAIN_ONLY=1, 18/11/2026) no longer scores any dev list, so this train-only output is enough; WP-D does not write dev arrays back.

#### Run checks (stage.json post_checks / verify; fixer 18/11/2026)

- post_check (fatal): `feats_screen2/feats_manifest.json` `files` = 6 entries: every v4-train and MAV-Celeb v1 / v2 array of the original run with its shape (6 arrays; values from package E ngnnguynchn__flag2027-feats-screen2-p.zip :: feats_manifest.json (created 2026-10-12 05:17), dev entries left out). A failed encoder (recorded in 'failed' and skipped) or a skipped MAV-Celeb source (ExtRows error only logged) leaves an array out, so the stage fails here instead of hours later downstream.
- post_check (fatal): `feats_screen2/feats_manifest.json` `external` = {"v1_complete": [7580, 19556], "v2_complete": [8953, 20715]}: MAV-Celeb v1 / v2 [face rows, voice rows] of the original manifest.
- post_check (fatal): `feats_screen2/feats_manifest.json` `splits/train` = 6485: organiser train rows.
- Comparisons the runner cannot express (row cosine, NaN pattern, finite-row masks) are kept as text in stage.json `verify_notes` for runner/verify.py / a person; they no longer sit in `verify` (where an entry without a file logged `WARN verify: missing None`).

#### Other differences from the original run (inputs, wiring, environment)

- **dev-exclusion**: ZIP_PREFIXES patch is an addition to the release patches (all lanes).
- **dev-exclusion** (environment, no code change): stage.json env pins `FLAG_SPLITS=train`. The original code lets FLAG_SPLITS override the patched SPLIT_FILTER in a smoke / local run; the runner applies the stage env after the caller's environment, so no outside value can turn dev extraction back on. Same value as the patched default: effect on numbers none.
- **dev-exclusion** (input view, pending runner support): the raw-v4 input asks the runner not to mount `dev_set.zip` / `dev_set` (stage.json inputs[].exclude). Today runner/ffcore.py build_vin applies only the configs/data_map.json exclude, so the archive is still visible; the code never unpacks or reads it (ZIP_PREFIXES / train-only index). With raw-v4 mounted unpacked (a hand-attached Kaggle dataset), find_splits still reads the dev list files (pd.read_csv of the list and an existence check of its media) before SPLIT_FILTER drops them; no dev clip or image is decoded or encoded. Mount raw-v4 as the zipped organiser release (FF_DATA/00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4).
- **dev-exclusion** (consequence of SPLIT_FILTER): the encoder self-checks (`self_check`: batch-vs-single and fp16-vs-fp32 cosine > 0.995 on the 3 longest clips of the first non-train split, else of train) now run on train clips; the original took a dev list. Effect on numbers: none (check only). Check behaviour on train clips: untested. A failing check puts the encoder in FAILED and skips it without stopping the run; the post_check on feats_manifest.json `files` below turns that into a stage failure.

#### Notes

- The RF core flag_final_core_092.py is embedded (zlib + base64) and checked by sha256 (25abe24d...); the ImageBind URL lives in the notebook's URL table, not in the core.
- Release's T2 run reproduced 16.18 with the corrected C1; that does not concern this stage (no LoRA here).

#### Run checks (stage.json post_checks / verify; fixer 18/11/2026)

- post_check (fatal): `feats_rfs_a/feats_manifest.json` `files` = 20 entries: every v4-train and MAV-Celeb v1 / v2 array of the original run with its shape (20 arrays; values from package E nguynchnngn1__flag2027-feats-rfs-a-p.zip :: feats_manifest.json (created 2026-11-03 16:38), dev entries left out). A failed encoder (recorded in 'failed' and skipped) or a skipped MAV-Celeb source (ExtRows error only logged) leaves an array out, so the stage fails here instead of hours later downstream.
- post_check (fatal): `feats_rfs_a/feats_manifest.json` `external` = {"v1_complete": [7580, 19556], "v2_complete": [8953, 20715]}: MAV-Celeb v1 / v2 [face rows, voice rows] of the original manifest.
- post_check (fatal): `feats_rfs_a/feats_manifest.json` `splits/train` = 6485: organiser train rows.
- Comparisons the runner cannot express (row cosine, NaN pattern, finite-row masks) are kept as text in stage.json `verify_notes` for runner/verify.py / a person; they no longer sit in `verify` (where an entry without a file logged `WARN verify: missing None`).

## cleancomp_tables

`stages/30_F_family/cleancomp_tables/CHANGES.md` (30_F_family, local-script)

### CHANGES: cleancomp_tables (local-script `Experiment/CLEANCOMP-01/build_tables.py` turned into a stage)

#### 1. Input read from the dataset folder instead of the local zip (type `path`; numbers: none)

- build_tables.py iterates over `kaggle/flag2027-e2e-dev.zip`; the stage iterates over the attached `e2edev/` folder (as the release stage
  `cleancomp_tables`, release/flag2027_e2e/stages.py:386-415). Same NEW columns, same alignment asserts, same CLEANCOMP.txt text. On Kaggle
  the three inner `ref/submission_v*.zip` are unpacked folders: they are copied as found (the original dataset flag2027-cleancomp-e2e-p shows the
  same unpacked layout). Parquet tables are re-serialised by `to_parquet` exactly as in build_tables.py.

#### 2. build_dev.py not run (type `dev-exclusion`; numbers: none for 13.54)

- `kaggle/cleancomp_chain.sh` also ran `Experiment/CLEANCOMP-01/build_dev.py` (local arrays kaggle/output/feats_ext_full, kaggle_upload,
  BEST/v6_eval/_members): it rewrote `components_dev.npz` (the four DEV lists scored with the strict XALL member and v11 head), added
  `CLEANCOMP_dev.txt` and a manifest note. This stage keeps the e2e-dev versions of these three files.
- Effect: only the dev scoring of t112sf1s (its dev zip) sees the old dev components; `ct01r2_final_models.pt`, the SAFE-04 tables used by the
  F1s' fuser (e50cc forwards `safe04/` only, kind == "e2e-dev"), and every Evaluation score are unaffected. The frozen dataset (with build_dev.py's
  files) stays the default for 13.54 (`skip_with`).

#### 3. Output layout and messages (type `path`; numbers: none)

- Output folder `ds/e2edev/` (the dataset = `ds/`, so the artifact has `e2edev/...` at its root like the original zip; the release stage exported
  the `e2edev` folder itself as the root). Readers locate the bundle by `e2e_manifest.json`, so both layouts work.
- English cell title; one extra `print` of the inputs and of the file count.

## gather_g1

`stages/30_F_family/gather_g1/CHANGES.md` (30_F_family, new)

### CHANGES: gather_g1 (new stage = the local gather step `sat_push.sh 111sg`)

- **Original step:** `gather_tables ct01g1 <dataset> <3 runs>` (kaggle/sat_push.sh:52-66) downloaded `ct01g1_fold[0-9].parquet` of the three runs
  (`kapi.py output --pattern`), required exactly 5 files, zipped them as `tables/<name>` and created the private dataset `ngnnguynchn/flag2027-ct02s-g1-tables-p` (`kapi.py dscreate`).

  `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-ct02s-g1-tables-p` (same layout as the original dataset). Type `wiring` (download / upload replaced by artifacts); numbers: none.

## safe04_lab

`stages/30_F_family/safe04_lab/CHANGES.md` (30_F_family, new)

### CHANGES: safe04_lab (new stage; no Kaggle run of its own ever existed)

The SAFE-04 lab of October 2026 ran on the local CPU; its scratch folder was deleted on 1/11/2026, and the zip it was packed into
(`kaggle/flag2027-e2e-dev.zip`, sha256 `0257b1fe...`) is the only copy left. This stage re-runs the ORIGINAL scripts (code/, verbatim) at
level **full** and is the producer of `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-e2e-dev-p`. What differs from the October procedure and why:

1. **Script versions** (type `pin`; numbers: unverified, believed none). The October file versions are not preserved. code/ holds:
   (a) the versions embedded in the executed notebooks of 3/11 (FLAG_164 / FLAG_111 runs; `code/CODE_MANIFEST.json` 'source'): every
   one equals today's repository file except `Experiment/SAFE-04/stream_gate.py` (the repository version later gained an opt-in NOREL
   branch; the 3/11 version is used); (b) four files that no executed run embeds, copied from the repository working tree, where they
   are untracked (`??`) and of unknown date: `Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/run2.py` (produces cafuse_en2_fold*, run at this level),
   `kaggle/build_e2e_dev_bundle.py` (only `safe04_tables()` is called: it selects the bundle columns), `Experiment/STACK-02/export.py`
   and `Experiment/STACK-02/pools.py` (reference only, not run). Their sha256 are in `code/CODE_MANIFEST.json`; they are unverified
   against the October 2026 versions. The known changes since October are opt-in flags (LID_STRICT / MAV_DROP, NOREL, CLEANCOMP, BN42);
   the stage removes them from the environment, because the October tables predate them and the 13.54 chain used exactly those old
   columns.
2. **Level full only; the producer takes no frozen table** (type `wiring`; 2026-11-18 fix). Every table of `e2edev/safe04/`
   (streams, relonly_multi, relonly_g, t3enc01f, bigb01f, cafuse_en2 per fold, stack02_rows, fold_persons.json) comes from the scripts
   of this run. The only producer-less input is the dev wiring of the frozen bundle, a separate artifact
   `datasets/ff-frozen/flag2027-e2e-dev-wiring-p` (the 9 members components_dev.npz, e2e_manifest.json, fusers/*.json, ref/*.zip of
   `kaggle/flag2027-e2e-dev.zip`, sha256 per file in `stage.json` `dev_wiring`; class wiring, dev data). The stage refuses a wiring
   folder that holds `safe04/` tables. Outside smoke all five folds are required. Previously the stage took the whole frozen bundle and,
   at its default level core, kept the XALL_* / CTRL_* columns and the other tables frozen. Under NOREL=1 + CLEANCOMP=1 they feed no F1s' fuser input of 13.54 (cleancomp_tables replaces XALL_* / relc_fia_eb / relg_fia_eb / anP512_* / t3_relg; NOREL drops rel / relg / t3_relg); they feed the F0 English fuser (CTRL_ib / CTRL_age through EN-T3-01 table_en + cafuse_en2 in CLEANTUNE-01 fit_en_clean, the t112sf0s / e112f0sx zip step) and wiring checks (check_v11_fuser). Whether any 13.54 number uses the F0 English file is for WP-G to confirm (WP-E's reading: no, the FACTOR_ENFUSE English scores come from the Bangla fuser of F1s'). Either way a core output is partial and must not stand in for the bundle. `notebooks/ff/f12_eval_feats` is an input of
   this stage only (no longer of the cheap level).
3. **stack02_rows reduced to the five columns the bundle keeps** (type `wiring`; numbers: none). `Experiment/STACK-02/export.py` needs the
   tables of ten other experiments (pools.py TABLES); the bundle keeps only `fold, data, y, fp, vp` (build_e2e_dev_bundle.py:91).
   `ff_derive_stack02.py` computes them with export.py's fp formula and stream_gate.load's vp formula, and fold_persons.json with
   export.py's formula (`indent=0`, LF).
4. **Run order and arms set explicitly** (type `wiring`): T3ENC-01 with `T3_ARMS=B` (T3ENC-01 README line 124, the bundle has t3B_* +
   t3_rel / t3_relg), BIGB-01 with `AN_ARMS=P256,B2x,P512,P256x2,P1024` (the five arms of the bundle's bigb01f; arm B is absent from it).
   All scripts run as `python -u <script>` (as locally); BIGB-01 originally ran through lab_entry.py on a Kaggle GPU (same code path;
   lab_entry only deletes the ECAPA-6144 placeholder stream, which BIGB-01 never reads).
5. **Real ECAPA-6144 arrays from f12_eval_feats** (type `path`). lab-data holds `(rows, 0)` placeholders; CAFUSE-EN/run2.py needs the
   real arrays, which the October lab had in kaggle/output/feats_eval (FLAG_12). The stage links the real files from the FLAG_12-round
   stage.
6. **Dev wiring copied, never recomputed** (type `dev-exclusion`). components_dev.npz, fusers/, ref/ and e2e_manifest.json come byte
   for byte from the dev-wiring artifact (build_e2e_dev_bundle.components() reads dev features: not called). fusers/v11_bangla.json is
   used downstream only as the reference of `e2e_lib.check_v11_fuser()` (an assertion on SAFE-04 table rows). A new file
   `e2edev/SAFE04_REBUILT.json` records level, folds, completeness, per-table provenance and the in-stage checks.
7. **Smoke** (`FF_SMOKE=1`): fold 1 only, `L0_STEPS=10`, `L0_NETS=1` (= the smoke settings of the FLAG_164 runs); the output holds the
   tables of fold 1 only, is marked `complete: false` and skips the 37 110-pair assertion.

Every cell of stage.ipy is new and opens with `print("[cell] ...", flush=True)`; subprocess output is streamed line by line.
The cheap partial rebuild (level core, a former evidence stage `safe04_lab_core` with the helper `ff_core_streams.py`) is removed
from this repository (19/11/2026): no 13.54 number depends on it.

#### Dev wiring optional (numbers: none) - added 19/11/2026

- **Where:** cell 1 (input check) and cell 4 (bundle packaging) of `stage.ipy`; `safe04_lab` `stage.json` input `datasets/ff-frozen/flag2027-e2e-dev-wiring-p` gets `"optional": true` (and loses `resolve: saved_only`).
- **Old:** level full required the dev wiring (dev-list files of the frozen bundle: `components_dev.npz`, `e2e_manifest.json`, `fusers/`, `ref/`; no producing stage) and copied it into the bundle.
- **New:** attached, it is copied byte for byte as before; absent, the bundle gets `e2e_manifest.json` with `kind` e2e-dev, `split` dev, `files` [] and `dev_wiring` false, and no dev-list file.
- **Why:** lineage of the 13.54 zip from raw data, and no dev data in the 13.54 chain. Readers of the bundle: the F-family training stages (bundle search accepts `safe04/` since 19/11; they read `safe04/` and the manifest's `created`), `cleancomp_tables` (copies every file), `e50cc` (copies `safe04/` from the bundle whose manifest kind is e2e-dev), `t164f` (dev scoring skipped without `components_dev.npz`).

#### 5. Runner

Runner-level only (not a change of this stage's code): the runner replaces the strings /kaggle/input and /kaggle/working by the stage's own input view and working folder, and prints a `[cell] <stage> k/n` line before each cell (DESIGN 2.4).

Runner-level requirement (2026-11-18 fix; not a change of this stage's code): `stage.json` declares `runner_requirements.literal_kaggle_input: true`. The modules decoded from `CODE_B64` at run time (listed in `runner_requirements.why`, with file and line) are written to /kaggle/working/lab and find their inputs by scanning the LITERAL path /kaggle/input, which the string rewrite above cannot reach. With the flag, the runner points /kaggle/input at this stage's own input view on Linux (symlink for the duration of the stage; `FF_LITERAL_KAGGLE=mounted` when a container already mounts it). On Kaggle the runner cannot change /kaggle/input and only warns: run this stage in a session with exactly its inputs attached (as the original run). Without the flag a Linux / Docker run would fail the asserts (lid_strict.json, adapter arrays, feats_vox2b, CT blocks), and a Kaggle orchestrator session would let the modules pick the session's own attachments (e.g. a saved original instead of an artifact rebuilt in the session).

- **Effect:** none on `ct01g1_final_models.pt`: T3RECIPE-01 `final()` (embedded run.py lines 196-214) trains on the train / auxiliary rows and saves the models with torch.save before it calls `E.components()` (lines 215-224); with `{}` the loop scores nothing and `ct01g1_final_dev.npz` holds only `info`. The A0 audit is unchanged. `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=0` gives the executed behaviour (needs the dev-list arrays of un-patched t153b / t153c / f47b_screen2, or the frozen originals, and dev_set.zip).

Why: the lab link loop asserts that every OPTIONAL_DIRS folder listed for the running experiment is attached; feats_av_full (AudioCLIP arrays of datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p, no producing stage) is read only by the F0 English zip path (e112f0sx), which this stage never runs, so the folder becomes optional here. When `components_dev.npz` exists the same bundle is chosen as before.

  (patch `count` = 1). `neutralize_upload: ["FL3_UPLOAD"]` also tells the runner to keep the variable unset.
- **Why:** the run pushed its arrays to the private dataset `phanvnhtrng/flag2027-feats-ls-b3s-p` with kagglehub (cell 14: `UP = os.environ.get("FL3_UPLOAD", "")`;
  empty → `no upload`). In the reproduction the stage output becomes the artifact `datasets/phanvnhtrng/flag2027-feats-ls-b3s-p` (`exports`, the 13 files of the original dataset).
- **Effect:** none (cell 14 only uploads; it never changes an array). Same replacement as the release stage `t153b`
  (release/flag2027_e2e/stages.py `noup`), only the comment text is English.

#### 2. Train-only: no dev list is embedded (type `dev-exclusion`; numbers: none) - 2026-11-18 fix

- **Where:** code cell 2 (settings), two lines; patches 2 and 3 of `stage.json` (same text as WP-D's `f47b_screen2`).
- **Old → new:** `SPLIT_FILTER = None` → `SPLIT_FILTER = ["train"]`; `ZIP_PREFIXES = ("train_set", "dev_set", "eval", "test")` → `("train_set", "eval", "test")` (dev_set.zip is never unpacked).
- **Why:** DESIGN 2.8 (every stage of the chain reads only the train set). The executed run embedded the four dev lists with the trained adapter (log: gender/Bangla 1468, no_gender/Bangla 1406, gender/English 982, no_gender/English 1008 rows); their only readers were the dev scoring steps of t112sf0s / t112sf1s, which are now skipped by default (FF_TRAIN_ONLY, their CHANGES.md). e65xbs / e65xcs copy only the non-dev files of this artifact (stages/50_eval/e65xbs/stage.ipy line 7009).
- **Effect:** none on the adapter (trained on VoxCeleb2 + VoxBlink faces only, before any list is embedded) and none on the train / MAV-Celeb / VoxCeleb2 / VoxBlink arrays (each split is embedded on its own). The export loses the four dev-list arrays `face_clipB3_{gender,no_gender}_{English,Bangla}.npy` (the original dataset had 13 members). `self_check()` takes its six batching / fp16 check images from the train list instead of the first dev list (a numeric self-test only). To restore the executed behaviour, revert both patches (and run t112s* with FF_TRAIN_ONLY=0).

#### 3. Organiser data layout (type `path`; numbers: none expected, unverified)

- On Kaggle the organiser zips were auto-extracted (log: `.../flag2027-raw-v4-p/train_set/train_set/train_set/train_English.txt`). FF_DATA holds `00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4/train_set.zip` / `dev_set.zip`, so `find_splits` finds no list and the notebook's own `unpack_zips()` fallback extracts train_set.zip into WORKDIR/raw (removed at the end, cell 12). No code change; the fallback was never exercised by a recorded run: the first smoke run must show the split `train` with 6485 rows (`stage.json` verify).

#### 4. Not changed, on purpose

- Internet downloads of cell 06 / cell 03 (FaRL ep16 GitHub release, ModelScope WeSpeaker zips, `pip install git+https://github.com/openai/CLIP.git@d50d76d...`,
  HF `openai/clip-vit-large-patch14@32bd642`): kept verbatim (`compute.internet = true`); offline route in `stage.json` `flags.offline_option`.
- `!pip -q install ftfy regex` (cell 03) is unpinned in the executed notebook and kept verbatim (`pip_policy: original`); the versions must come from the environment specification (`stage.json` `pip_note`).
- Licence: the exports hold VoxBlink face features (`vb_face_clipB3.npy`) and an adapter trained on VoxBlink frames (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0): private FF_DATA only, never in git (`stage.json` `licence`).

#### 5. Runner

Runner-level only (not a change of this stage's code): the runner replaces the strings /kaggle/input and /kaggle/working by the stage's own input view and working folder, and prints a `[cell] <stage> k/n` line before each cell (DESIGN 2.4).

  the pushed md5). #### 3. Runner

Runner-level only (not a change of this stage's code): the runner replaces the strings /kaggle/input and /kaggle/working by the stage's own input view and working folder, and prints a `[cell] <stage> k/n` line before each cell (DESIGN 2.4).

Runner-level requirement (2026-11-18 fix; not a change of this stage's code): `stage.json` declares `runner_requirements.literal_kaggle_input: true`. The modules decoded from `CODE_B64` at run time (listed in `runner_requirements.why`, with file and line) are written to /kaggle/working/lab and find their inputs by scanning the LITERAL path /kaggle/input, which the string rewrite above cannot reach. With the flag, the runner points /kaggle/input at this stage's own input view on Linux (symlink for the duration of the stage; `FF_LITERAL_KAGGLE=mounted` when a container already mounts it). On Kaggle the runner cannot change /kaggle/input and only warns: run this stage in a session with exactly its inputs attached (as the original run). Without the flag a Linux / Docker run would fail the asserts (lid_strict.json, adapter arrays, feats_vox2b, CT blocks), and a Kaggle orchestrator session would let the modules pick the session's own attachments (e.g. a saved original instead of an artifact rebuilt in the session).

#### Requirement 1: frozen wiring inputs removed (19/11/2026 (requirement-1 closure))

- input `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-cc5-oof-p` dropped (stage.json `inputs_dropped`): attached in the original run but never read by this stage: its tables (t3recipe02_fold*.parquet) are read only by DSEL-02/run.py with_cc5() and DSEL-03/run.py attach(), which are reached only from the DSEL-02 / DSEL-03 / SCALE-CC-01 / AUXP-01 report commands; this stage runs CLEANTUNE-01 / FACTOR-01 / FUSENET-01 commands, which never call them, and no cell checks the dataset's presence. No producing stage exists (old run FLAG_57).
- input `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-dsel02-oof-p` dropped (stage.json `inputs_dropped`): attached in the original run but never read by this stage: its tables (dsel02_fold*.parquet) are read only by DSEL-02/run.py and DSEL-03/run.py report paths, which this stage's commands never call; no presence check. No producing stage exists (old run FLAG_58).
- input `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-facelora03-tables-p` dropped (stage.json `inputs_dropped`): attached in the original run; real runs only copy facelora03_B3_fold*.parquet into SP/out/safe04 (no assert) and nothing reads the copies: the only reader is the smoke stand-in (FL3Z_SMOKE / FLAG_SMOKE), which now takes the key columns of the SAFE-04 streams tables instead (patch of the same date). No producing stage exists (old runs FLAG_86 / 86b).
- input `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p` dropped (stage.json `inputs_dropped`): attached in the original run only to satisfy the lab presence check OPTIONAL_DIRS['Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full'] = ['CLEANTUNE-01']; its content (AudioCLIP arrays) is read only by EN-T3-01 table_en() via CLEANTUNE-01 fit_en_clean(), called from the zip command when F0_ZIP is unset, i.e. only in e112f0sx (F0 English). The presence check is made optional by the patch of the same date. No producing stage exists (team package labavfull/, FLAG_32 run 2).
- patch (smoke): `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"facelora03_B3_fold{s}.parquet")` -> `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"streams_fold{s}.parquet")[["fold", "data", "protocol", "y", "fi", "vi", "XALL_deep0"]].rename(columns={"XALL_deep0": "trB3"})`. Why: smoke only (FL3Z_SMOKE / FLAG_SMOKE): the stand-in tables were noisy copies of FACELORA-03's B3 tables (datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-facelora03-tables-p, no producing stage). They now start from the SAFE-04 streams table of the same fold (frozen bundle / stage safe04_lab, already in SP/out/safe04): same 7 406 rows and the same key columns fold, data, protocol, y, fi, vi (checked on fold 1 against the team copies, 19/11); XALL_deep0 plays the role of trB3, whose columns the stand-in drops anyway. Effect on numbers: none in real runs (smoke stand-ins only)
- patch (wiring): `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": ["CLEANTUNE-01"]}` -> `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": []}`. Why: the lab link loop asserts that every OPTIONAL_DIRS folder listed for the running experiment is attached; feats_av_full (AudioCLIP arrays of datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p, no producing stage) is read only by the F0 English zip path (e112f0sx), which this stage never runs, so the folder becomes optional here. Effect on numbers: none (the folder is never opened by this stage's commands)
- evidence: `repo_map/build/REQ1_GAPS.md` (team archive); `python runner/check_stage.py` rebuilds this stage

#### SAFE-04 bundle without dev wiring accepted (type `wiring`; numbers: none) - added 19/11/2026

- **Where:** the e2e bundle search (`e2e = [... components_{SPLIT}.npz ...]`).
- **Old:** a bundle qualified only with `components_dev.npz` (dev-list arrays of the frozen dev wiring, no producing stage).
- **New:** a bundle with a `safe04/` folder qualifies too. Stage `safe04_lab` writes such a bundle (manifest without dev lists) when the dev wiring is not attached.
- **Why:** lineage of the 13.54 zip from raw data. With `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=1` (default) this stage reads only `safe04/` and the manifest's `created` field; the dev scoring that reads `components_dev.npz` runs only with `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=0`. When `components_dev.npz` exists the same bundle is chosen as before.

- patch (smoke): `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"facelora03_B3_fold{s}.parquet")` -> `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"streams_fold{s}.parquet")[["fold", "data", "protocol", "y", "fi", "vi", "XALL_deep0"]].rename(columns={"XALL_deep0": "trB3"})`. Why: smoke only (FL3Z_SMOKE / FLAG_SMOKE): the stand-in tables were noisy copies of FACELORA-03's B3 tables (datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-facelora03-tables-p, no producing stage). Effect on numbers: none in real runs (smoke stand-ins only)
- patch (wiring): `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": ["CLEANTUNE-01"]}` -> `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": []}`. Why: the lab link loop asserts that every OPTIONAL_DIRS folder listed for the running experiment is attached; feats_av_full (AudioCLIP arrays of datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p, no producing stage) is read only by the F0 English zip path (e112f0sx), which this stage never runs, so the folder becomes optional here. Effect on numbers: none (the folder is never opened by this stage's commands)
- evidence: `repo_map/build/REQ1_GAPS.md` (team archive); `python runner/check_stage.py` rebuilds this stage

#### SAFE-04 bundle without dev wiring accepted (type `wiring`; numbers: none) - added 19/11/2026

- **Where:** the e2e bundle search (`e2e = [... components_{SPLIT}.npz ...]`).
- **Old:** a bundle qualified only with `components_dev.npz` (dev-list arrays of the frozen dev wiring, no producing stage).
- **New:** a bundle with a `safe04/` folder qualifies too. Stage `safe04_lab` writes such a bundle (manifest without dev lists) when the dev wiring is not attached.
- **Why:** lineage of the 13.54 zip from raw data. With `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=1` (default) this stage reads only `safe04/` and the manifest's `created` field; the dev scoring that reads `components_dev.npz` runs only with `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=0`. When `components_dev.npz` exists the same bundle is chosen as before.

With the flag, the runner points /kaggle/input at this stage's own input view on Linux (symlink for the duration of the stage; `FF_LITERAL_KAGGLE=mounted` when a container already mounts it). On Kaggle the runner cannot change /kaggle/input and only warns: run this stage in a session with exactly its inputs attached (as the original run). Without the flag a Linux / Docker run would fail the asserts (lid_strict.json, adapter arrays, feats_vox2b, CT blocks), and a Kaggle orchestrator session would let the modules pick the session's own attachments (e.g. a saved original instead of an artifact rebuilt in the session).

On Kaggle the runner cannot change /kaggle/input and only warns: run this stage in a session with exactly its inputs attached (as the original run). Without the flag a Linux / Docker run would fail the asserts (lid_strict.json, adapter arrays, feats_vox2b, CT blocks), and a Kaggle orchestrator session would let the modules pick the session's own attachments (e.g. a saved original instead of an artifact rebuilt in the session).

#### C. Kept as in the original run (on purpose)

- Inputs: exactly the datasets / kernels the run attached (`stage.json` -> `inputs`, from the run's kernel metadata), including the ones attached but not read.
- Every other line of every cell, the embedded code (`original/decoded/`, byte-exact, CRLF kept) and the `[boot]` / `[cell]` lines that kapi inserted at push time.
- pip calls (`pip_policy: original`).
- The ORIGINAL LoRA C1 (`onnhqunhh/flag2027-rfs-lora-p`, `lora_C1.pt` sha256 `b97372e3...`); the substitutions to the fixed C1 made for the 16.18 system (`release/flag2027_e2e/stages.py:234-320, 526-527`) and the `lorafix1` defaults of today's scripts are NOT applied (see `../C1_VARIANTS.md`).

#### D. Changes in `stage.json` outside the code (fixer, 18/11/2026, review of WP-F)

- `runner_requirements.literal_kaggle_input` = `false` (new key; why: the only literal /kaggle/input read in the decoded code is rfs_train.py _xload (line 83 / 96), redirected to the stage's input view by the RFS_XDIRS path patch of the setup cell; every other path is in the stage text (rewritten by the runner) or passed as an argument (grep of original/decoded/: flag_final_core_092.py mentions /kaggle/input in a docstring only)).
- `post_checks`: removed `{"file": "rfs_art/lora_C1.pt", "sha256": "b97372e3..."}`. The runner reads every post_check as a JSON file (runner/ffcore.py verify_stage), so this entry failed fatally on the binary torch file of every real run. The same hash stays in `verify` (check `exact`) and is now also enforced on the input (next items).
- `inputs` -> `datasets/onnhqunhh/flag2027-rfs-lora-p`: `"resolve": "saved_only"` (runner: only a saved / attached copy counts, whatever FF_MODE / FF_USE_SAVED say; the producer t163l_orig is not scheduled for it). `variant.require_files`: `lora_C1.pt` sha256 `b97372e3...` on that input (runner build_vin, before the stage runs, smoke included). Reason: in mode (a) a re-trained C1 can never equal b97372e3 (GPU), and 13.54 is defined by the original file (DESIGN 2.7); see ../README.md 'LoRA C1 policy'.
- `compute.gpus` 1 -> 2 (+ `gpus_note`): the runs had Kaggle T4 x2 (logs 'gpus 2', machine_shape NvidiaTeslaT4); the code uses one device, so numbers do not depend on it.

#### E. Input datasets/trngsnam/flag2027-feats-peface01-pe0-p dropped (stage.json only; integration fixer r1, 18/11/2026)

- type: `wiring`
- where: stage.json `inputs` (entry removed; recorded under `inputs_dropped`); no code change
- old: attached as in the original run (only indexed by the .npy scan of `_xload`)
- new: not attached
- why: INTEGRATION_r1 M7 / B3. Never read: `_xload` (decoded rfs_train.py:83) selects files by exact name for the requested blocks only (RFS_CFG {"add": ["f_c5s", "v_rl"]}); the dataset holds only `*_peL*.npy` (block `f_pel`), `bn42_clean.json`, `facelora_state.pt`, `train_log.json`. Same drop as WP-H (t163p_fvb, t189_b2). It has no producing stage, so it was a requirement-1 gap with no use.
- effect on numbers: none

- patch (smoke): `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"facelora03_B3_fold{s}.parquet")` -> `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"streams_fold{s}.parquet")[["fold", "data", "protocol", "y", "fi", "vi", "XALL_deep0"]].rename(columns={"XALL_deep0": "trB3"})`. Why: smoke only (FL3Z_SMOKE / FLAG_SMOKE): the stand-in tables were noisy copies of FACELORA-03's B3 tables (datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-facelora03-tables-p, no producing stage). They now start from the SAFE-04 streams table of the same fold (frozen bundle / stage safe04_lab, already in SP/out/safe04): same 7 406 rows and the same key columns fold, data, protocol, y, fi, vi (checked on fold 1 against the team copies, 19/11); XALL_deep0 plays the role of trB3, whose columns the stand-in drops anyway. Effect on numbers: none in real runs (smoke stand-ins only)
- patch (wiring): `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": ["CLEANTUNE-01"]}` -> `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": []}`. Why: the lab link loop asserts that every OPTIONAL_DIRS folder listed for the running experiment is attached; feats_av_full (AudioCLIP arrays of datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p, no producing stage) is read only by the F0 English zip path (e112f0sx), which this stage never runs, so the folder becomes optional here. Effect on numbers: none (the folder is never opened by this stage's commands)
- evidence: `repo_map/build/REQ1_GAPS.md` (team archive); `python runner/check_stage.py` rebuilds this stage

#### SAFE-04 bundle without dev wiring accepted (type `wiring`; numbers: none) - added 19/11/2026

- **Where:** the e2e bundle search (`e2e = [... components_{SPLIT}.npz ...]`).
- **Old:** a bundle qualified only with `components_dev.npz` (dev-list arrays of the frozen dev wiring, no producing stage).
- **New:** a bundle with a `safe04/` folder qualifies too. Stage `safe04_lab` writes such a bundle (manifest without dev lists) when the dev wiring is not attached.
- **Why:** lineage of the 13.54 zip from raw data. With `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=1` (default) this stage reads only `safe04/` and the manifest's `created` field; the dev scoring that reads `components_dev.npz` runs only with `FF_TRAIN_ONLY=0`. When `components_dev.npz` exists the same bundle is chosen as before.

## t3s01_trca

`stages/40_R_family/t3s01_trca/CHANGES.md` (40_R_family, new)

### CHANGES: t3s01_trca (new stage, integration fixer r1, 18/11/2026)

- **Why the stage exists (INTEGRATION_r1 B3, user requirement 1):** `t219_dumpv4` (source of sigma 5.0983 / 3.8376) mounted the frozen dataset
  `ngnnguynchn/flag2027-t3s01-tables-p`, which no stage of the repository produced. Its producing runs (FLAG_167 T3S-01, five fold runs, then the
  gather step `kaggle/sat_push.sh 167g`) cannot be rebuilt: their staging folders are empty and only the builder `kaggle/FLAG_167_t3s01.ipynb` is left.
- **What it makes:** the four job tables per fold that `CLEANTUNE-01/run.py merge_t3s` expects (`ct01r2{b3s,c5s,clip,farl}_fold<s>.parquet`), with the
  row keys `fold, data, protocol, y, fi, vi` of `ct02s-r2ca-tables-p` and, in the farl tables only, its `trCA` column (as in the frozen dataset).
- **Difference from the frozen dataset (type `wiring`):** the 24 `trT3S_*` branch columns are absent. The dumpv4 path of `FUSENET-01/run.py report`
  fuses `F1S = S6 + V11_T3 + ["trCA"]` and RFX only; `trT3S_*` feed `reportt3s` / `final_t3s` / `_with_t3s`, which t219 does not call.
- **Effect on numbers:** none expected for sigma (trCA identical: WP-F light check 18/11, max |diff| 0.0 on all five folds; the stage re-checks it when the
  frozen copy is present).
- **Consumers:** `t219_dumpv4` (input `datasets/ff/flag2027-t3s01-trca-p`, mounted at `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-t3s01-tables-p`). No other stage reads the
  frozen dataset.

- input `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p` dropped (stage.json `inputs_dropped`): attached in the original run only to satisfy the lab presence check OPTIONAL_DIRS['Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full'] = ['CLEANTUNE-01']; its content (AudioCLIP arrays) is read only by EN-T3-01 table_en() via CLEANTUNE-01 fit_en_clean(), called from the zip command when F0_ZIP is unset, i.e. only in e112f0sx (F0 English). The presence check is made optional by the patch of the same date. No producing stage exists (team package labavfull/, FLAG_32 run 2).
- patch (smoke): `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"facelora03_B3_fold{s}.parquet")` -> `pd.read_parquet(SP / "out/safe04" / f"streams_fold{s}.parquet")[["fold", "data", "protocol", "y", "fi", "vi", "XALL_deep0"]].rename(columns={"XALL_deep0": "trB3"})`. Effect on numbers: none in real runs (smoke stand-ins only)
- patch (wiring): `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": ["CLEANTUNE-01"]}` -> `"Experiment/CAFUSE-EN/feats_av_full": []}`. Why: the lab link loop asserts that every OPTIONAL_DIRS folder listed for the running experiment is attached; feats_av_full (AudioCLIP arrays of datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-lab-avfull-p, no producing stage) is read only by the F0 English zip path (e112f0sx), which this stage never runs, so the folder becomes optional here. ### e65xcs: changes from the original run

**One code change** (dev-exclusion, fix round 18/11). `stage.ipy` = the 13 code cells of `original/02_flag-2027-nb-65xcs-c5sx-eval.ipynb` with the `stage.json` patch `no_dev_zip` (sha256 `e948aca445dc5d1a1b577973b550745a62c46e2a3070c5fef76b1faadd11f6ec`). Without it, the code cells are byte-identical to `release/flag2027_e2e/code/e65xcs.ipy` (sha256 `88fc960d7509dcbea17fca44c0227b9df653d08f57f29abb18f3012ccda26e97`, the code of the run). No patch of `release/flag2027_e2e/stages.py` applies (its global ImageBind URL patch has no hit in this notebook).

| # | where | old → new | why | effect on numbers | type |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | code cell 1/13 "01 what to run" (`stage.ipy` line 21) | `ZIP_PREFIXES = ("train_set", "dev_set", "eval", "test")` → `ZIP_PREFIXES = ("train_set", "eval", "test")` plus a comment (a `stage.json` patch, id `no_dev_zip`, `count` 1, `in_release: false`; same text as the patch of `f47b_screen2`) | the repository mounts `datasets/ngnnguynchn/flag2027-raw-v4-p` as the organisers' files in `FF_DATA/00_raw/btc_mavceleb_v4` (`train_set.zip`, `dev_set.zip`, meta csv), not as the tree Kaggle had auto-extracted for the original run (log: `…/flag2027-raw-v4-p/train_set/train_set/train_set/train_English.txt`, no `unpacking` line). No train list is then found, so the verbatim fallback `unpack_zips()` runs, and without the patch it unpacks `dev_set.zip` too (9 757 members, ~990 MB) into `/kaggle/working/raw`. With the patch the dev archive is never opened (DESIGN §2.8). Found by the WP-G review of 18/11. | none: `train_set.zip` is unpacked by the same fallback (same train list and media as the auto-extracted copy, other path; the log gains an `unpacking …/train_set.zip` line) and the verbatim last cell removes `raw/` (`shutil.rmtree(WORKDIR / "raw")`), so the output holds no organiser media; dev lists were already skipped by `ONLY_UNDER` | dev-exclusion |

Original run: `thanhhuyen11/flag-2027-nb-65xcs-c5sx-eval`, last push 11/11/2026 22:05 (UTC+7) of a retry loop that pushed the same
notebook nine times (kapi_runs lines 1468, 1469, 1471–1473, 1476, 1479–1481, all md5 `e79a5615742b2320a13cfb19c2f09d62`); the
team-package copy in `original/` has the same code cells.

| item | decision | effect on numbers |
|---|---|---|

| inputs | the nine datasets of the run, as pushed (`original/kernel-metadata.json`) | none |
| dev data | `ONLY_UNDER = 'evaluation_phase'` (code cell 01): dev lists are not read; with change 1, `dev_set.zip` is never opened | none |
| output content | the output folder holds every file copied from the adapter dataset (code cell 10), including the VoxBlink face array `vb_face_clip*.npy` and `facelora_state.pt` (adapter learned with VoxBlink frames): declared in `stage.json` `outputs.contains` (`voxblink: true`); the run output is private storage only. ## a_fvbind

`stages/60_fvbind/a_fvbind/CHANGES.md` (60_fvbind, local-script)

### CHANGES: a_fvbind

Kind local-script: the original steps were two shell commands on the team machine (no notebook). The stage code (stage.ipy) is new driver code; the logic is the six scripts under tree/, byte-exact copies of the team repository files.

Every change against the code of the original run (fields: type, where, old, new, why, effect on numbers). The runner also maps `/kaggle/input` and `/kaggle/working` to the stage's own folders and prints a `[cell]` line before every cell; neither touches the code below.

#### 1. scripts run from a scratch copy of the original folder layout instead of the team repository root

- type: `path`
- where: stage.ipy cell 1
- old: the scripts and their inputs at fixed relative paths of the team repository (Experiment/..., release/...)
- new: the driver copies the six scripts and the six inputs to the same relative paths in a temporary folder and runs the same commands there
- why: keeps every script byte-exact (no path parameterisation needed) while making the stage independent of the team machine
- effect on numbers: none

#### 2. stub Experiment/POSTEVAL-01/manifest.json in the scratch folder

- type: `wiring`
- where: stage.ipy cell 1
- old: ENFUSE-U/run.py appended the sha256 of ENF_OUT to the team's POSTEVAL-01 manifest
- new: it appends to an empty manifest {"zips": {}} in the scratch folder (copied to logs/ for audit)
- why: the script needs the file to exist when ENF_OUT is set; the team manifest is not an input of anything in the chain
- effect on numbers: none

#### 3. optional knobs removed from the environment

- type: `variant`
- where: stage.ipy cell 1 (ENV)
- old: XT_W / ENF_W did not exist at the time of the build (added 15/11 09:33)
- new: XT_W, ENF_W (and stray ENF_RFS / ENF_U / ENF_OUT) are popped before each command
- why: with both unset the scripts compute the 1 : 1 sums of the 15/11 build
- effect on numbers: none

#### 4. added one-step cross-check and member sha256 checks

- type: `save`
- where: stage.ipy cells 4-5
- old: -
- new: rebuild_final.py with rfx_eval.zip := rfsfxfvb_eval.zip; sha256 of every member against the 15/11 zips; a_fvbind_report.json
- why: the one-step script is the shorter route to the same zip; agreement is evidence that the reconstruction of the two hand-run commands is right; the chain output (cells 2-3) is the one kept
- effect on numbers: none

#### 5. saved score files also searched in <repo>/assembly/inputs

- type: `path`
- where: stage.ipy cell 1, function flat_roots() (+ the FileNotFoundError message of find())
- old: $A_FVBIND_INPUTS, then <FF_DATA root>/05_scores, then a search of the stage's input view
- new: the same, with <repo>/assembly/inputs added after the 05_scores folders (repo = three levels above the stage folder FF_STAGE_DIR)
- why: DESIGN 5.3 names assembly/inputs/ as the place of the saved score files in a private copy of the repository (decision Q-D); assembly/assemble_exact.py already searches it
- effect on numbers: none (the input sha256 are checked as before)

#### Not changed (deliberately)

- The arithmetic, pair order and %.8f printing of XTEAM-03/run.py, ENFUSE-U/run.py and rebuild_final.py.
- The --counts of the U command (392 / 802 / 416 / 716) as in the original command.
- Zip timestamps are the build time, so whole-zip sha256 differ from the originals; members are compared.

#### 1. artifact folder pinned

- type: `wiring`

- new: `pip('--no-deps', 'git+https://github.com/facebookresearch/ImageBind.git@53680b02d7e37b19b124fa37bae4b6c98c38f5be')`

- effect on numbers: none expected

- Checkpoint URLs (HF mirror of ImageBind pinned to a commit; resnet221 / voxblink2 'resolve/main' and the Oxford VGG-Face URL) kept: every download is checked against the sha256 table of the config cell.
- pip('--no-deps', '-U', 'speechbrain>=1.1.1') and the other conditional installs kept (environment pinning: docker/).
- CORE_SOURCE (the RF core, sha256 3cad7b8a...) untouched in stage.ipy: the run prints its sha256.

- where: CORE_SOURCE string literal (stage.ipy line 43), one occurrence of `/kaggle/input` in the docstring of locate_roots (= original/embedded/code/flag_e2e_core.py:1220, '... a recursive search of /kaggle/input with the 28.9 GB external tree ...')

- effect: FLAG_RF_E2E/code/flag_e2e_core.py, the printed 'Code SHA256' and run_report.json code_sha256 of a runner run differ from 3cad7b8a...; the expected line 'Code SHA256: 3cad7b8a...' in stage.json log_expectations is therefore marked 'original run only'

## t163p_fvb

`stages/60_fvbind/t163p_fvb/CHANGES.md` (60_fvbind, exact-staging)

### CHANGES: t163p_fvb

Stage code = the 6 code cells of the exact notebook thanhhuyen11/flag-2027-nb-163-rfsfxfvb-p (kapi_runs:1605, md5 308d8715ee7d...).

Every change against the code of the original run (fields: type, where, old, new, why, effect on numbers). The runner also maps `/kaggle/input` and `/kaggle/working` to the stage's own folders and prints a `[cell]` line before every cell. The mapping does not touch the code below except through change 4 (the embedded loader reads `RFS_XDIRS`, which the mapping rewrites).

#### 1. organisers' data removed from the repository copy of the notebook

- type: `organiser-data-exclusion`
- where: original/flag-2027-nb-163-rfsfxfvb-p.redacted.ipynb, setup cell, tuple ("train/gender.csv", <base64>) (byte offset 5436 of the exact file)
- old: a 228-character gzip + base64 literal = the organisers' meta_file_train_set.csv (sha256 fd7991a3...)
- new: `""` (empty string): the cell writes an empty rfs/train/gender.csv
- why: user rule 6: no organisers' data in the repository. The literal is exactly gzip.compress(csv, compresslevel=9, mtime=0) in base64, so `python stages/60_fvbind/restore_exact.py --csv <meta_file_train_set.csv> --out <dir>` rebuilds the byte-exact notebook (md5 checked)
- effect on numbers: none: only assemble.py (RFS_MODE=B, stage t163b) reads train/gender.csv; this stage never runs mode B

#### 2. upload to Kaggle disabled

- type: `upload`
- where: kapi parameter cell (notebook cell 0 = stage.ipy cell 0)
- old: `os.environ['RFS_UPLOAD'] = 'thanhhuyen11/flag2027-rfsfxfvb-art-p'`
- new: a comment line (see the patch in stage.json)
- why: the runner never uploads to Kaggle; rfs_art/ becomes the artifact datasets/thanhhuyen11/flag2027-rfsfxfvb-art-p through stage.json exports; with RFS_UPLOAD unset the upload cell prints and skips
- effect on numbers: none

#### 3. ImageBind code pinned to a commit

- type: `pin`
- where: setup cell (notebook cell 2 = stage.ipy cell 1; RFS_MODE=L branch); 1 occurrence; inert in this run (mode P never executes it), pinned for a uniform rule
- old: `pip -q install --no-deps git+https://github.com/facebookresearch/ImageBind`
- new: `pip -q install --no-deps git+https://github.com/facebookresearch/ImageBind@53680b02d7e37b19b124fa37bae4b6c98c38f5be`
- why: the run installed GitHub HEAD without a commit; 53680b02d7e37b19b124fa37bae4b6c98c38f5be is main on 2026-11-05 (release/docker/Dockerfile:22-23). NOT VERIFIED that HEAD was unchanged on 14-15/11; the model weights are fixed by the sha256 check of imagebind_huge.pth
- effect on numbers: none expected

#### 4. RFS_XDIRS set to the literal input root, so the runner's path mapping reaches the embedded loader

- type: `path`
- where: kapi parameter cell (notebook cell 0 = stage.ipy cell 0), one line added after `os.environ['RFS_CFG'] = '{"add": ["f_c5s", "v_rl", "v_fvb"]}'` (patch 2 of stage.json)
- old: (no line) RFS_XDIRS unset
- new: `os.environ['RFS_XDIRS'] = '/kaggle/input'` + comment
- why: `_xload` of the gzip+base64-embedded rfs_train.py (original/embedded/train/rfs_train.py:89) scans `RFS_XDIRS` or else the literal `/kaggle/input` for the FACTOR-01 extra blocks (f_c5s, v_rl, v_fvb, reached through load_extra for RFS_CFG add, rfs_train.py:521 / 547 / 565 / 707 (mode P and its selfcheck)). The runner rewrites `/kaggle/input` only in the text of stage.ipy, never inside code decoded at run time, and this stage does not declare `runner_requirements.literal_kaggle_input`. Without the line, on Linux / Windows the blocks are not found (AssertionError 'FACTOR-01: ... not found'); in a Kaggle orchestrator session the loader would index the whole notebook input (every attached FF_DATA folder, but not artifacts made earlier in the same session under FF_WORK) and merge same-name copies row-wise. With the line, the runner's rewrite turns the value into the stage's own input view, which holds exactly the inputs of the original run; the subprocesses (`run()` / fvbind.py) inherit `os.environ`.
- effect on numbers: none. On Kaggle the value equals the default root of `_xload`, so the original notebook behaves the same; under the runner the loader sees the same files as the original run.

#### 5. input datasets/trngsnam/flag2027-feats-peface01-pe0-p dropped

- type: `wiring`
- where: stage.json inputs (no code change); the dataset stays in original/kernel-metadata.json as evidence of the original attachment
- old: attached (only indexed by the .npy scan of `_xload`)
- new: not attached; recorded under `inputs_dropped` in stage.json
- why: never read. `_xload` selects files by exact name (vox_face_clipC5.npy, voice_rn293.npy, voice_ecapa1024.npy, voice_fvb.npy, ...) and this dataset holds only `*_peL*.npy` (block f_pel, not requested by this stage), `bn42_clean.json`, `facelora_state.pt` and `train_log.json` (13 members, listing of E:/FLAG2027_submit/FACTOR_ENFUSE_c5rl/datasets/trngsnam__flag2027-feats-peface01-pe0-p.zip); no cell of the stage looks these names up (`find_all` asks only ext.npz, v4.npz, lid_strict.json, ct01r2_fold1.parquet, lora_C1.pt, v4_extra.npz, tar0000.npz). It has no producing stage (configs/data_map.json class wiring, producer null: a walk back to raw data could not pass it, user requirement 1) and it holds Evaluation-list features (face_peL_{gender,no_gender}_*.npy) that a training stage has no reason to mount.
- effect on numbers: none

#### 6. smoke branch uses the mounted original LoRA C1 instead of a stand-in

- type: `variant`
- where: inputs cell (notebook cell 3 = stage.ipy cell 2), `elif SMOKE:` branch, the line `torch.save({"params": [torch.zeros(2)]}, WORK / "lora_C1.pt")` (patch 3 of stage.json)
- old: the smoke branch always wrote a stand-in lora_C1.pt
- new: it copies the first mounted lora_C1.pt (the original C1 of datasets/onnhqunhh/flag2027-rfs-lora-p); the stand-in only when none is mounted
- why: stage_release copies work/lora_C1.pt into rfs_art/lora_C1.pt (the exported artifact); the runner's variant file check refuses any lora_C1.pt that is not the original C1, so the declared smoke run could never pass. Mode P only copies the file, never loads it. Same patch as stage t163p_c5rl (WP-F)
- effect on numbers: none (FLAG_SMOKE=1 path only)

#### 7. runner requirement declared: literal /kaggle/input not needed (stage.json only; integration fixer r1, 18/11/2026)

- type: `path`
- where: stage.json `runner_requirements = {"literal_kaggle_input": false, "why": ...}`
- old: no declaration; the runner's payload scan found the literal root of `rfs_train.py _xload` and treated the stage as literal (refused on Windows, separate Kaggle session needed)
- new: explicit `false` with the reason; runner/ffcore.py `is_literal` now honours an explicit, reasoned `false` (`FF_LITERAL_HONOUR_FALSE=0` restores detection)
- why: INTEGRATION_r1 M1. The only literal use is `_xload` (embedded rfs_train.py:89), redirected by the RFS_XDIRS patch (change 4).
- effect on numbers: none

#### 8. original LoRA C1 input resolved as saved only (stage.json only; integration fixer r1, 18/11/2026)

- type: `variant`
- where: stage.json input `datasets/onnhqunhh/flag2027-rfs-lora-p`: `"resolve": "saved_only"` (no code change)
- old: the input followed configs/data_map.json `default_saved`; with `FF_FORCE=t163l_orig` the runner preferred the C1 retrained in the same session, so RFX' (this chain) could get a different C1 than RFX (WP-F stages use `saved_only`)
- new: always the saved ORIGINAL C1 (`FF_DATA/03_checkpoints/rfs_lora_c1_original`, `lora_C1.pt` b97372e3...), exactly as t163p_c5rl, t213_p*, t216_p3
- why: INTEGRATION_r1 M2 (one C1 for both Bangla files of 13.54)
- effect on numbers: none for the 13.54 chain (the original run attached this very dataset)

#### Not changed (deliberately)

- RFS_MODE=P and RFS_CFG={"add": ["f_c5s", "v_rl", "v_fvb"]} (kapi parameter cell) kept verbatim.
- Inputs kept as attached (except the never-read peface01 dataset, change 5), including the original LoRA C1 dataset onnhqunhh/flag2027-rfs-lora-p (variant 13.54) and the datasets that are only indexed; kaggle/rfx_chain.sh (LORAD line 11 = fixed C1, no FLAG_192 kernel on the P push) is NOT used.
